## Data-Catalog-App Exploration Summary

I've thoroughly explored the data-catalog-app directory. Here's what I found:

### 1. Framework & Tech Stack
- **Framework**: **Astro 6.1.3** (latest static/hybrid site generator)
- **Hosting**: Deployed on **Cloudflare Workers** via `@astrojs/cloudflare` adapter
- **Package Manager**: **pnpm**
- **Node Version Required**: >=22.12.0
- **Environment**: TypeScript with Astro strict config

### 2. UI Components Currently Used
The app is extremely minimal, containing only basic demo components:
- **Welcome.astro** - A welcome/hero component with gradient text, logo, and call-to-action buttons
- **Layout.astro** - Basic HTML layout wrapper with minimal styling
- **Custom SVG elements** - Background blur effect, Astro logo, Discord icon

**No external UI component libraries are currently installed** (no Material-UI, Shadcn, Tailwind, etc.)

### 3. Overall Structure

```
data-catalog-app/
├── src/
│   ├── assets/           # SVG graphics (astro.svg, background.svg)
│   ├── components/       # Astro components (Welcome.astro)
│   ├── layouts/          # Page layouts (Layout.astro)
│   └── pages/            # Routes (index.astro - home page)
├── public/               # Static assets (favicons)
├── dist/                 # Built output (production)
├── .astro/               # Generated types & config
├── .wrangler/            # Cloudflare Worker build artifacts
├── astro.config.mjs      # Astro configuration
├── wrangler.jsonc        # Cloudflare Worker config
├── tsconfig.json         # TypeScript config
├── package.json          # Dependencies
└── pnpm-lock.yaml        # Lockfile
```

### 4. Key Dependencies

**Production Dependencies:**
- `astro@^6.1.3` - Web framework
- `@astrojs/cloudflare@^13.1.7` - Cloudflare adapter
- `wrangler@^4.80.0` - Cloudflare CLI tool

**Overrides:**
- `vite@^7` - Build tool override

### 5. Key Files

- **astro.config.mjs** - Minimal config with Cloudflare adapter
- **wrangler.jsonc** - Worker config with:
  - Name: "flame"
  - Observability enabled
  - Assets binding pointing to `./dist`
  - Compatibility date: 2026-04-04
- **tsconfig.json** - Extends Astro strict preset

### Current State
This is a **fresh Astro starter template** that hasn't been customized yet. It's deployed as a serverless application on Cloudflare Workers. The app is completely boilerplate with no UI framework, no styling framework, and no application logic. It's ready for development but awaits implementation of the actual data catalog functionality.

## Summary of Search Results

Kumo is Cloudflare's component library for building modern web applications, and the @cloudflare/kumo package is published to the public npm registry.

**Key Features:**

- Kumo provides accessible, design-system-compliant UI components built on Base UI, handling keyboard navigation, focus management, and ARIA attributes
- It's a UI component library that helps you build accessible, responsive web apps with React and Tailwind CSS
- The current version is v1.10.0

**Components and Blocks:**

- It offers 35+ components that work seamlessly together
- Components like Button, Input, and Dialog are core UI primitives published as NPM exports
- Blocks are higher-level compositions (like PageHeader and ResourceListPage) that are installed via the Kumo CLI

**Installation and Import:**

You can install Kumo using your preferred package manager and import components either from the main package or use granular imports for better tree-shaking. The npm package name is `@cloudflare/kumo`.

You can find the official documentation at [kumo-ui.com](https://kumo-ui.com/) and the GitHub repository at [github.com/cloudflare/kumo](https://github.com/cloudflare/kumo).


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Based on the search results, here's what I found about Cloudflare Kumo and its relationship with Astro and React:

## Kumo Overview

Kumo is a UI component library from Cloudflare that helps you build accessible, responsive web apps with React and Tailwind CSS. It combines the accessibility primitives of Base UI with Cloudflare's design system, offering 35+ components that work seamlessly together.

## Astro Integration

The Kumo monorepo contains a component library (Base UI + Tailwind v4), an Astro docs site, and a Figma plugin. Specifically, the documentation site uses Astro with interactive component demos in the kumo-docs-astro package.

## React Components

Kumo provides accessible, design-system-compliant UI components built on Base UI. It handles keyboard navigation, focus management, and ARIA attributes so you can build accessible applications without thinking through every detail.

Kumo includes a built-in CLI for quick component reference, which reads from ai/component-registry.json (generated from TypeScript types + demo examples).

## Installation and Usage

Components can be imported using main package imports or granular imports (recommended for tree-shaking). Important: Tailwind CSS v4 does not scan node_modules/ by default, so you must add a @source directive so Tailwind can discover the utility classes used by Kumo components.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

I've found comprehensive information about Cloudflare Kumo installation with React and Tailwind setup. Here are the key details:

## Installation Basics

The @cloudflare/kumo package is published to the public npm registry with no special configuration required, and you can install Kumo using your preferred package manager. The current version is v1.10.0.

## Tailwind CSS Setup

Tailwind CSS v4 does not scan node_modules/ by default, and you must add a @source directive so Tailwind can discover the utility classes used by Kumo components. In your app.css or main.css file, add the @source directive pointing to `../node_modules/@cloudflare/kumo/dist/**/*.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}` and import the Kumo styles before the tailwindcss import.

## Component Imports

Kumo requires peer dependencies including react, react-dom, and @phosphor-icons/react, which most React projects will already have installed. You can import components from the main package or use granular imports for better tree-shaking.

## CSS Distribution Options

If your application uses Tailwind CSS, add Kumo's source files to your content configuration and import the styles. Alternatively, if your application doesn't use Tailwind CSS, you can use the standalone build which includes all compiled styles by importing it in your app entry point.

For more detailed information and examples, you can visit the official installation guide at kumo-ui.com.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

# Kumo Component Library: Installation & Setup Guide

## Core Installation

The primary package installation command is:
```
pnpm add @cloudflare/kumo
```

## Required Peer Dependencies

You must also install these peer packages:
```
pnpm add react react-dom @phosphor-icons/react
```

## CSS Configuration

### For Tailwind CSS Users (v4)

Add this to your main CSS file (e.g., `app.css`):

```css
@source "../node_modules/@cloudflare/kumo/dist/**//*.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}";
@import "@cloudflare/kumo/styles/tailwind";
@import "tailwindcss";
```

**Critical Note:** "Import order matters — @cloudflare/kumo/styles must come before @import "tailwindcss" so Kumo's @theme tokens are registered first."

The `@source` directive enables Tailwind v4 to discover utility classes from the library. Adjust the path based on your project structure.

### For Non-Tailwind Projects (Standalone CSS)

Use the pre-compiled standalone build:

```javascript
import "@cloudflare/kumo/styles/standalone";
```

This includes "all Tailwind utility classes used by Kumo components" plus animations and responsive utilities—no configuration needed.

## Component Imports

**Main entry point:**
```typescript
import { Button, Input, Surface } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
```

**Granular/tree-shaking imports (recommended):**
```typescript
import { Button } from "@cloudflare/kumo/components/button";
```

## Accessing Component Documentation

Use the built-in CLI tool:

```
npx @cloudflare/kumo ls        # List all components
npx @cloudflare/kumo doc Button # Get specific documentation
npx @cloudflare/kumo docs       # Get all component docs
```

## Base UI Primitives

The library bundles Base UI primitives for custom use cases. Import via granular paths for optimal bundling:

```typescript
import { Popover } from "@cloudflare/kumo/primitives/popover";
import { Slider } from "@cloudflare/kumo/primitives/slider";
```
```

# Kumo Component Library: Setup & Usage Guide

## Overview

Kumo is Cloudflare's React component library built on Base UI and Tailwind v4. The monorepo includes the component library, documentation site, Figma plugin, and screenshot worker.

## Installation & Setup

### Prerequisites

- Node.js: `^24.12.0`
- pnpm: `>=10.21.0`
- TypeScript: `5.9.2`

### Project Structure

The repository organizes packages as follows:
- `packages/kumo/` - Main component library
- `packages/kumo-docs-astro/` - Documentation site
- `packages/kumo-figma/` - Figma plugin
- `packages/kumo-screenshot-worker/` - Visual regression testing

## Core Commands

```bash
# Development
pnpm dev                                    # Start docs server (localhost:4321)
pnpm lint                                   # Run oxlint + custom rules
pnpm typecheck                              # Check all packages
pnpm changeset                              # Create changeset (required for changes)

# Component library
pnpm --filter @cloudflare/kumo build        # Build library
pnpm --filter @cloudflare/kumo test         # Run Vitest
pnpm --filter @cloudflare/kumo codegen:registry  # Regenerate registry
```

## Component Creation

Generate new components using the scaffolding tool:

```bash
pnpm --filter @cloudflare/kumo new:component
```

Never create component files manually—the tool maintains required vite, package.json, and index updates.

## Styling Conventions (Critical)

### Semantic Tokens Only

Always use semantic tokens for theming:
- `bg-kumo-base`, `text-kumo-default`, `border-kumo-line`, `ring-kumo-ring`

### Prohibited Patterns

- Raw Tailwind colors (`bg-blue-500`, `text-gray-900`) fail linting
- `dark:` variants are unnecessary; "dark mode is automatic via `light-dark()`"
- Manual styling breaks theming

### Valid Exceptions

Whitelist: `bg-white`, `bg-black`, `text-white`, `text-black`, `transparent`

### Class Composition

```javascript
cn("base", conditional && "extra", className)
```

## Component API Reference

Access the authoritative registry at:

`packages/kumo/ai/component-registry.{json,md}`

Query with standard tools like `jq` or command-line utilities.

## Important Notes

- Blocks in `src/blocks/` install via CLI (`kumo add`), not direct imports
- Semantic tokens auto-generate from `scripts/theme-generator/config.ts`
- 40 Base UI re-exports exist in `src/primitives/`
- Never edit auto-generated files directly

## Changeset Requirements

Changesets are mandatory for `packages/kumo/` changes (enforced pre-push). Optional for docs site. Prohibited actions: `pnpm version`, `pnpm release`, `pnpm publish:beta`, `pnpm release:production`.

## Build Pipeline

Documentation demos feed into registry codegen, which then supplies Figma plugin data generation. Run codegen steps in dependency order.
```

Kumo Components (39 total)

Action:
  Button - Primary action trigger. Supports multiple variants, sizes, shapes, icons, and loading state.
  ClipboardText - Read-only text field with a one-click copy-to-clipboard button.

Display:
  Badge - Small status label for categorizing or highlighting content.
  Breadcrumbs - Breadcrumbs component
  Code - Code component
  Collapsible - Collapsible component for showing/hiding content.  Features: - Animated chevron indicator (rotates 180° when open) - Accessible with aria-expanded and aria-controls - Content panel with left border accent
  Empty - Placeholder shown when a list, table, or page has no content to display.
  LayerCard - LayerCard component
  Meter - Progress bar showing a measured value within a known range (e.g. quota usage).
  Text - Text component

Feedback:
  Banner - Full-width message bar for informational, warning, or error notices. Supports structured title/description for i18n, or simple children for basic usage.
  Loader - Animated circular spinner for indicating loading states.
  Toasty - Toasty — toast notification provider and viewport.  Renders a `Toast.Provider` with a fixed-position viewport in the bottom-right corner. Toasts stack with smooth enter/exit animations, swipe-to-dismiss, and expand-on-hover.  Built on `@base-ui/react/toast`.

Input:
  Checkbox - Checkbox component
  Combobox - Combobox — autocomplete input with filterable dropdown list.  Compound component: `Combobox` (Root), `.TriggerInput`, `.TriggerValue`, `.TriggerMultipleWithInput`, `.Content`, `.Item`, `.Chip`, `.Input`, `.Empty`, `.GroupLabel`, `.Group`, `.List`, `.Collection`.
  DateRangePicker - DateRangePicker — dual-calendar date range selector.  Renders two side-by-side month calendars with click-to-select start/end dates, hover preview of the range, a timezone footer, and a reset button.
  Field - Form field wrapper that provides a label, optional description, and error display around any form control. Built on Base UI Field primitives.
  Input - Input component
  InputArea - Multi-line textarea input with Input variants and InputArea-specific dimensions
  Radio - Radio — radio button group for single-select choices.  Compound component: `Radio.Group` (with built-in Fieldset) and `Radio.Item`. Built on `@base-ui/react/radio-group` + `@base-ui/react/radio`.
  Select - Select component
  Switch - Switch component

Layout:
  Grid - Responsive CSS grid layout container with preset column configurations.
  Surface - Polymorphic container with consistent background, shadow, and border styling.  Use the `render` prop to change the underlying element: ```tsx <Surface render={<section />} className="rounded-lg p-4">Card content</Surface> ```

Navigation:
  CommandPalette - CommandPalette — accessible command palette / spotlight search overlay.  Compound component: `CommandPalette.Root` (or `.Dialog` + `.Panel`), `.Input`, `.List`, `.Results`, `.Items`, `.Group`, `.GroupLabel`, `.Item`, `.ResultItem`, `.HighlightedText`, `.Empty`, `.Loading`, `.Footer`.  Built on `@base-ui/react/autocomplete` + `@base-ui/react/dialog`.
  MenuBar - MenuBar — horizontal icon-button toolbar with keyboard arrow-key navigation.  Each option renders as a `<button>` with a Tooltip. The active option is visually highlighted with an elevated background.
  Pagination - Pagination component
  Tabs - Tab navigation component with segmented or underline style. Built on Base UI Tabs with animated active indicator.

Other:
  CloudflareLogo - Cloudflare logo component.
  DatePicker - DatePicker — a date selection calendar.  Built on [react-day-picker](https://daypicker.dev) with Kumo styling. Supports three selection modes: single, multiple, and range.
  Label - Label component for form fields.  Provides a standardized way to display labels with optional indicators: - Optional indicator: gray "(optional)" text when `showOptional={true}` - Tooltip: info icon with hover tooltip for additional context
  Link - Link component
  SensitiveInput - Password/secret input that masks its value by default and reveals on click. Includes a built-in copy-to-clipboard button on hover.
  Sidebar - Sidebar — responsive navigation panel with expand/collapse support.  Compound component: `Sidebar` (root `<aside>`), `.Provider`, `.Header`, `.Content`, `.Footer`, `.Group`, `.GroupLabel`, `.GroupContent`, `.Menu`, `.MenuItem`, `.MenuButton`, `.MenuAction`, `.MenuBadge`, `.MenuSub`, `.MenuSubItem`, `.MenuSubButton`, `.Separator`, `.Input`, `.Trigger`, `.Rail`, `.MenuChevron`, `.Collapsible`, `.CollapsibleTrigger`, `.CollapsibleContent`.  Built on `@base-ui/react/collapsible` + `@base-ui/react/dialog`.
  Table - Table — semantic HTML table with styled rows, cells, and selection support.  Compound component: `Table` (Root), `.Header`, `.Head`, `.Body`, `.Row`, `.Cell`, `.Footer`, `.CheckCell`, `.CheckHead`, `.ResizeHandle`.

Overlay:
  Dialog - Dialog component
  DropdownMenu - DropdownMenu — accessible dropdown menu anchored to a trigger.  Compound component: `DropdownMenu` (Root), `.Trigger`, `.Content`, `.Item`, `.LinkItem`, `.CheckboxItem`, `.RadioGroup`, `.RadioItem`, `.RadioItemIndicator`, `.Sub`, `.SubTrigger`, `.SubContent`, `.Label`, `.Separator`, `.Shortcut`, `.Group`.  Built on `@base-ui/react/menu`.
  Popover - Popover component for displaying accessible popup content anchored to a trigger.
  Tooltip - Accessible popup that shows additional information on hover/focus. Wrap your app or section with `<TooltipProvider>` to enable delay grouping.
```

# Sidebar

Sidebar — responsive navigation panel with expand/collapse support.  Compound component: `Sidebar` (root `<aside>`), `.Provider`, `.Header`, `.Content`, `.Footer`, `.Group`, `.GroupLabel`, `.GroupContent`, `.Menu`, `.MenuItem`, `.MenuButton`, `.MenuAction`, `.MenuBadge`, `.MenuSub`, `.MenuSubItem`, `.MenuSubButton`, `.Separator`, `.Input`, `.Trigger`, `.Rail`, `.MenuChevron`, `.Collapsible`, `.CollapsibleTrigger`, `.CollapsibleContent`.  Built on `@base-ui/react/collapsible` + `@base-ui/react/dialog`.

**Import:** `import { Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Other

## Props

defaultOpen: boolean
    Initial open state when uncontrolled.

open: boolean
    Controlled open state.

variant: "sidebar" | "floating" | "inset"  [default: sidebar]
    Sidebar layout variant.
    - "sidebar": Standard sidebar with border separator
    - "floating": Floating sidebar with shadow and rounded corners
    - "inset": Inset sidebar within the content area

side: "left" | "right"  [default: left]
    Which side the sidebar is on.
    - "left": Left-aligned sidebar
    - "right": Right-aligned sidebar

collapsible: "icon" | "offcanvas" | "none"  [default: icon]
    - "icon": Collapses to show icons only
    - "offcanvas": Slides off screen when collapsed
    - "none": Cannot be collapsed

resizable: boolean
    Enable drag-to-resize on the sidebar edge.

defaultWidth: number
    Initial width in pixels when resizable.

minWidth: number
    Minimum width in pixels when resizing.

maxWidth: number
    Maximum width in pixels when resizing.

children: ReactNode
    Content — typically `<Sidebar>` + main content.

className: string
    Additional CSS classes for the wrapper div.

## Sub-Components

### Sidebar.Provider
Provider sub-component
Props:
  - defaultOpen: boolean 
  - open: boolean 
  - variant: SidebarVariant 
  - side: SidebarSide 
  - collapsible: "icon" | "offcanvas" | "none" 
  - resizable: boolean 
  - defaultWidth: number 
  - minWidth: number 
  - maxWidth: number 
  - children: ReactNode (required)
  - className: string 

### Sidebar.Header
Header sub-component
Props:

### Sidebar.Content
Content sub-component
Props:

### Sidebar.Footer
Footer sub-component
Props:

### Sidebar.Group

# Tabs

Tab navigation component with segmented or underline style. Built on Base UI Tabs with animated active indicator.

**Import:** `import { Tabs } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Navigation

## Props

tabs: TabsItem[]
    Array of tab items to render.

value: string
    Controlled value. When set, component becomes controlled.

selectedValue: string
    Default selected value for uncontrolled mode. Ignored when `value` is set.

activateOnFocus: boolean
    When `true`, tabs are activated immediately upon receiving focus via arrow keys. When `false` (default), tabs receive focus but require Enter/Space to activate.

className: string
    Additional CSS classes for the root element.

listClassName: string
    Additional CSS classes for the tab list element.

indicatorClassName: string
    Additional CSS classes for the indicator element.

variant: "segmented" | "underline"  [default: segmented]
    Tab style.
- `"segmented"` — Pill-shaped indicator on a filled track
- `"underline"` — Underline indicator below tab text

onValueChange: (value: string) => void
    Callback when active tab changes

## Examples

```tsx
<div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div>
        <p className="mb-2 text-sm text-kumo-subtle">Segmented (default)</p>
        <Tabs
          variant="segmented"
          tabs={[
            { value: "tab1", label: "Tab 1" },
            { value: "tab2", label: "Tab 2" },
            { value: "tab3", label: "Tab 3" },
          ]}
          selectedValue="tab1"
        />
      </div>
      <div>
        <p className="mb-2 text-sm text-kumo-subtle">Underline</p>
        <Tabs
          variant="underline"
          tabs={[
```

# Table

Table — semantic HTML table with styled rows, cells, and selection support.  Compound component: `Table` (Root), `.Header`, `.Head`, `.Body`, `.Row`, `.Cell`, `.Footer`, `.CheckCell`, `.CheckHead`, `.ResizeHandle`.

**Category:** Other

## Props

layout: "auto" | "fixed"  [default: auto]
    - "auto": Auto table layout - columns resize based on content
    - "fixed": Fixed table layout - columns have equal width, controlled via colgroup

variant: "default" | "selected"  [default: default]
    - "default": Default row variant
    - "selected": Selected row variant

sticky: "left" | "right"
    - "left": Pin column to the left edge of the scroll container
    - "right": Pin column to the right edge of the scroll container

className: string

children: ReactNode

# Badge

Small status label for categorizing or highlighting content.

**Import:** `import { Badge } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Display

## Props

variant: "red" | "red-subtle" | "orange" | "orange-subtle" | "yellow" | "yellow-subtle" | "green" | "green-subtle" | "teal" | "teal-subtle" | "blue" | "blue-subtle" | "neutral" | "neutral-subtle" | "inverted" | "outline" | "beta" | "primary" | "secondary" | "destructive" | "success"  [default: neutral]
    Color variant of the badge.
- `"red"` / `"red-subtle"` — Red badge
- `"orange"` / `"orange-subtle"` — Orange badge
- `"yellow"` / `"yellow-subtle"` — Yellow badge
- `"green"` / `"green-subtle"` — Green badge (emerald scale)
- `"teal"` / `"teal-subtle"` — Teal badge
- `"blue"` / `"blue-subtle"` — Blue badge
- `"neutral"` / `"neutral-subtle"` — Neutral badge
- `"inverted"` — Inverted badge (near-black, white in dark mode)
- `"outline"` — Bordered badge with transparent background
- `"beta"` — Dashed-border badge for beta/experimental features
- `"primary"` — **Deprecated.** Use `"inverted"` instead.
- `"secondary"` — **Deprecated.** Use `"neutral"` instead.
- `"destructive"` — **Deprecated.** Use `"red"` instead.
- `"success"` — **Deprecated.** Use `"green"` instead.
    - "red": Red badge
    - "red-subtle": Subtle red badge
    - "orange": Orange badge
    - "orange-subtle": Subtle orange badge
    - "yellow": Yellow badge
    - "yellow-subtle": Subtle yellow badge
    - "green": Green badge
    - "green-subtle": Subtle green badge
    - "teal": Teal badge
    - "teal-subtle": Subtle teal badge
    - "blue": Blue badge
    - "blue-subtle": Subtle blue badge
    - "neutral": Neutral badge
    - "neutral-subtle": Subtle neutral badge

# Input

Input component

**Import:** `import { Input } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Input

## Props

label: ReactNode
    Label content for the input (enables Field wrapper) - can be a string or any React node

labelTooltip: ReactNode
    Tooltip content to display next to the label via an info icon

description: ReactNode
    Helper text displayed below the input

error: string | object
    Error message or validation error object

size: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg"  [default: base]
    Input size.
- `"xs"` — Extra small for compact UIs
- `"sm"` — Small for secondary fields
- `"base"` — Default size
- `"lg"` — Large for prominent fields
    - "xs": Extra small input for compact UIs
    - "sm": Small input for secondary fields
    - "base": Default input size
    - "lg": Large input for prominent fields

variant: "default" | "error"  [default: default]
    Visual variant.
- `"default"` — Standard input
- `"error"` — Error state for validation failures
    - "default": Default input appearance
    - "error": Error state for validation failures
```

# Button

Primary action trigger. Supports multiple variants, sizes, shapes, icons, and loading state.

**Import:** `import { Button } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Action

## Props

shape: "base" | "square" | "circle"  [default: base]
    - "base": Default rectangular button shape
    - "square": Square button for icon-only actions
    - "circle": Circular button for icon-only actions

size: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg"  [default: base]
    - "xs": Extra small button for compact UIs
    - "sm": Small button for secondary actions
    - "base": Default button size
    - "lg": Large button for primary CTAs

variant: "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "destructive" | "secondary-destructive" | "outline"  [default: secondary]
    - "primary": High-emphasis button for primary actions
    - "secondary": Default button style for most actions
    - "ghost": Minimal button with no background
    - "destructive": Danger button for destructive actions like delete
    - "secondary-destructive": Secondary button with destructive text for less prominent dangerous actions
    - "outline": Bordered button with transparent background

children: ReactNode

className: string

icon: ReactNode
    Icon from `@phosphor-icons/react` or a React element. Rendered before children.

loading: boolean
    Shows a loading spinner and disables interaction.

title: string

id: string

lang: string

disabled: boolean

name: string

type: "submit" | "reset" | "button"
```

# Input

Input component

**Import:** `import { Input } from "@cloudflare/kumo";`

**Category:** Input

## Props

label: ReactNode
    Label content for the input (enables Field wrapper) - can be a string or any React node

labelTooltip: ReactNode
    Tooltip content to display next to the label via an info icon

description: ReactNode
    Helper text displayed below the input

error: string | object
    Error message or validation error object

size: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg"  [default: base]
    Input size.
- `"xs"` — Extra small for compact UIs
- `"sm"` — Small for secondary fields
- `"base"` — Default size
- `"lg"` — Large for prominent fields
    - "xs": Extra small input for compact UIs
    - "sm": Small input for secondary fields
    - "base": Default input size
    - "lg": Large input for prominent fields

variant: "default" | "error"  [default: default]
    Visual variant.
- `"default"` — Standard input
- `"error"` — Error state for validation failures
    - "default": Default input appearance
    - "error": Error state for validation failures

## Examples

```tsx
<Input
      label="Email"
      placeholder="you@example.com"
      description="We'll never share your email"
    />
```

```tsx
<Input
      label="Username"
      placeholder="Choose a username"
      description="3-20 characters, alphanumeric only"
    />
```

```tsx
<Input
      label="Email"
      placeholder="you@example.com"
      value="invalid-email"
      variant="error"
      error="Please enter a valid email address"
    />
```

(8 more examples available in Storybook)

## Semantic Tokens Used

bg-kumo-control, ring-kumo-danger, ring-kumo-line, ring-kumo-ring, text-kumo-default, text-kumo-subtle
```

> AGENT

完了です。以下の変更を行いました:

**インストールしたパッケージ:**
- `@cloudflare/kumo` - Cloudflare Kumoコンポーネントライブラリ
- `@astrojs/react` - AstroのReact統合
- `@tailwindcss/vite` + `tailwindcss` - Tailwind CSS v4
- `react`, `react-dom`, `@phosphor-icons/react` - Kumoのpeer dependencies
- `@types/react`, `@types/react-dom` - 型定義

**作成・変更したファイル:**
- `astro.config.mjs` - React integration + Tailwind v4 Vite plugin追加
- `src/styles/global.css` - Kumo + Tailwind CSSの設定（`@source`ディレクティブ含む）
- `src/layouts/Layout.astro` - タイトル「Flame - Data Catalog」、Kumoのセマンティックトークン適用
- `src/components/AppSidebar.tsx` - Kumo `Sidebar` を使ったナビゲーション
- `src/components/DataCatalog.tsx` - Kumoの `Table`, `Badge`, `Button`, `Tabs`, `Input`, `Surface`, `Text` を使ったデータカタログUI
- `src/pages/index.astro` - 新しいコンポーネント構成

**削除したファイル:**
- `Welcome.astro`, `astro.svg`, `background.svg`（ボイラープレート）

`pnpm dev` で http://localhost:4321 にアクセスすると確認できます。

Sources:
- [Kumo公式サイト](https://kumo-ui.com/)
- [Kumoインストールガイド](https://kumo-ui.com/installation/)
- [GitHub - cloudflare/kumo](https://github.com/cloudflare/kumo)
- [@cloudflare/kumo - npm](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@cloudflare/kumo)

`astro.config.mjs` uses `@astrojs/react`, but `package.json` does not declare `@astrojs/react` in dependencies/devDependencies. This will fail installs/builds unless the dependency is added explicitly.

[Copilot] data-catalog-app/package.json:19
This app imports React (`useState`) and `@phosphor-icons/react` directly, but they are not listed in `package.json`. Relying on pnpm `autoInstallPeers`/transitive deps is brittle; add `react`, `react-dom`, and `@phosphor-icons/react` explicitly to avoid install/runtime issues in different environments.
```suggestion
    "@cloudflare/kumo": "^1.17.0",
    "@phosphor-icons/react": "^2.1.7",
    "@tailwindcss/vite": "^4.2.2",
    "astro": "^6.1.3",
    "react": "^18.3.1",
    "react-dom": "^18.3.1",
```

`MagnifyingGlass` is imported but never used in this component, which will typically fail linting/TS settings (or at least add noise). Remove the unused import or use it (e.g., as an `Input` icon) to keep the file clean.

import { Plus, ArrowsClockwise } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
```

[Copilot] data-catalog-app/pnpm-workspace.yaml:4
This `pnpm-workspace.yaml` doesn’t define the required `packages:` globs and may be treated as an invalid workspace manifest by pnpm (breaking `pnpm install` in the test plan). If the goal is to allow certain postinstall builds, use the appropriate pnpm config (e.g., `onlyBuiltDependencies`/`.npmrc`) rather than a workspace manifest, or add a valid `packages:` section.
```suggestion
packages:
  - '.'

onlyBuiltDependencies:
  - esbuild
  - sharp
  - workerd
```

[gemini-code-assist[bot]] data-catalog-app/src/components/DataCatalog.tsx:62
![high](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/high-priority.svg)

現在のフィルタリングロジックは検索語しか考慮しておらず、選択されたタブが反映されていません。UIが示す完全なフィルタリング機能を提供するために、これを更新する必要があります。この変更は、選択されたタブを保持するstate変数（例：`const [selectedTab, setSelectedTab] = useState('all');`）に依存します。また、検索語を一度だけ小文字に変換する小さな最適化も含まれています。

```
  const lowercasedSearch = search.toLowerCase();
  const filtered = datasets.filter(
    (d) =>
      (selectedTab === "all" || d.type === selectedTab) &&
      (d.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowercasedSearch) ||
        d.source.toLowerCase().includes(lowercasedSearch))
  );
```
---
[gemini-code-assist[bot]] data-catalog-app/src/components/DataCatalog.tsx:92
![high](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/high-priority.svg)

`Tabs`コンポーネントは現在静的です。機能させるためには、state変数に接続する必要があります。また、フィルタリングを容易にするために、タブの`value`を`datasets`の`type`プロパティと一致させるべきです。データには存在するもののUIにはなかった「Time Series」タブも追加しました。

この変更は、コンポーネントに`const [selectedTab, setSelectedTab] = useState('all');`のようなstate変数を追加したことを前提としています。

```
      <Tabs
        variant="underline"
        tabs={[
          { value: "all", label: "All Datasets" },
          { value: "Table", label: "Tables" },
          { value: "View", label: "Views" },
          { value: "Key-Value", label: "Key-Value" },
          { value: "Time Series", label: "Time Series" },
        ]}
        selectedValue={selectedTab}
        onValueChange={setSelectedTab}
      />
```

![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)

この`README.md`はAstroのデフォルトテンプレートのままのようです。「Flame」データカタログアプリに特化した情報に更新することを強く推奨します。良いREADMEには通常、以下の内容が含まれます：

70	            Browse and discover datasets across your data platform
71	          </Text>
72	        </div>
73	        <div className="flex gap-2">
74	          <Button variant="secondary" icon={<ArrowsClockwise size={16} />}>
75	            Refresh
76	          </Button>

79	          </Button>

92	      />

96	          <Input
97	            placeholder="Search datasets..."
98	            value={search}
99	            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
100	          />

123	                <Table.Cell>{dataset.source}</Table.Cell>

131	                </Table.Cell>
132	              </Table.Row>

9	## 🚀 Project Structure
10	
11	Inside of your Astro project, you'll see the following folders and files:
12	
13	```text
14	/
15	├── public/
16	│   └── favicon.svg
17	├── src
18	│   ├── assets
19	│   │   └── astro.svg
20	│   ├── components
21	│   │   └── Welcome.astro
22	│   ├── layouts
23	│   │   └── Layout.astro
24	│   └── pages
25	│       └── index.astro
26	└── package.json
27	```
28	
29	To learn more about the folder structure of an Astro project, refer to [our guide on project structure](https://docs.astro.build/en/basics/project-structure/).
30	
31	## 🧞 Commands
32	
33	All commands are run from the root of the project, from a terminal:
34	
35	| Command                   | Action                                           |
36	| :------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------- |
37	| `pnpm install`             | Installs dependencies                            |
38	| `pnpm dev`             | Starts local dev server at `localhost:4321`      |
39	| `pnpm build`           | Build your production site to `./dist/`          |
40	| `pnpm preview`         | Preview your build locally, before deploying     |
41	| `pnpm astro ...`       | Run CLI commands like `astro add`, `astro check` |
42	| `pnpm astro -- --help` | Get help using the Astro CLI                     |
43	
44	## 👀 Want to learn more?
45	
46	Feel free to check [our documentation](https://docs.astro.build) or jump into our [Discord server](https://astro.build/chat).

  "content": "# Flame - Data Catalog\n\nCloudflare Workers上にデプロイされるデータカタログアプリケーション。\nデータプラットフォーム全体のデータセットを閲覧・検索できるUIを提供します。\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n- **Astro 6** - Webフレームワーク\n- **React 19** - UIコンポーネント\n- **Cloudflare Kumo** - UIコンポーネントライブラリ\n- **Tailwind CSS v4** - スタイリング\n- **Cloudflare Workers** - デプロイ先\n\n## Project Structure\n\n```text\ndata-catalog-app/\n├── public/\n│   └── favicon.svg\n├── src/\n│   ├── components/\n│   │   ├── AppSidebar.tsx      # Kumo Sidebar ナビゲーション\n│   │   └── DataCatalog.tsx     # データセット一覧・検索UI\n│   ├── layouts/\n│   │   └── Layout.astro        # ベースHTMLレイアウト\n│   ├── pages/\n│   │   └── index.astro         # ホームページ\n│   └── styles/\n│       └── global.css          # Kumo + Tailwind CSS設定\n├── astro.config.mjs            # Astro設定\n├── wrangler.jsonc              # Cloudflare Workers設定\n└── package.json\n```\n\n## Getting Started\n\n```bash\npnpm install\npnpm dev        # localhost:4321 で起動\n```\n\n## Commands\n\n| Command          | Action                              |\n| :--------------- | :---------------------------------- |\n| `pnpm install`   | 依存関係をインストール              |\n| `pnpm dev`       | 開発サーバーを起動 (`localhost:4321`) |\n| `pnpm build`     | プロダクションビルド (`./dist/`)     |\n| `pnpm preview`   | ビルド結果をローカルでプレビュー    |\n"
}

## Pull request overview

Adds a new standalone Astro-based “Flame” data catalog app under `data-catalog-app/`, built with React, Tailwind v4, and Cloudflare Kumo UI components, intended to be deployable to Cloudflare Workers via the Astro Cloudflare adapter.

**Changes:**
- Introduces the Astro + React app skeleton (layout, page, Kumo-based sidebar + catalog table UI, global styles).
- Adds Cloudflare Workers deployment config (`wrangler.jsonc`) and TypeScript config for strict Astro typing.
-

{"baseRefName":"main","body":"## Summary\n\n- **AI Gateway**: 全 Workers AI 呼び出しをゲートウェイ経由に変更（ログ/キャッシュ/レート制限）\n- **Vectorize + RAG**: `bge-base-en-v1.5` 埋め込みモデルで健康データインサイトを意味検索可能に\n- **R2**: 生成レポートを `reports/` プレフィックスで data-lake バケットに保存\n- **D1 拡張**: `agent_knowledge_base`, `agent_reports`, `agent_conversation_logs` テーブル追加\n- **新ツール 6 種**: semanticSearch, generateReport, listReports, readReport, saveInsight, listDataLakeFiles\n- **Cron**: 毎日 9:00 UTC に前日インサイト生成、月曜に週次サマリー自動生成\n- **テスト**: 30 テスト (utils, embeddings, knowledge, reports)\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nUser (Chat / Slack)\n  │\n  ▼\nDataAgent (Durable Object)\n  ├── Workers AI ──→ AI Gateway (ログ/キャッシュ/レート制限)\n  │     ├── Llama 3.3 70B (チャット/分析)\n  │     └── bge-base-en-v1.5 (埋め込み)\n  ├── Vectorize (RAG: 768次元 cosine 意味検索)\n  ├── D1 \"raw\" (構造化データ + エージェントテーブル)\n  └── R2 \"data-lake\" (レポート保存 + データレイク参照)\n```\n\n## Deploy prerequisites\n\n1. `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`\n2. `wrangler d1 migrations apply raw`\n3. `pulumi up` (AI Gateway リソース作成)\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `pnpm test:run` — 30 tests passing\n- [x] `tsc --noEmit` — no type errors\n- [x] `biome check .` — no lint issues\n- [ ] Deploy to dev and verify chat RAG flow\n- [ ] Verify Slack integration with RAG context\n- [ ] Confirm AI Gateway dashboard shows request logs\n- [ ] Test cron-triggered insight generation\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n  * Semantic search, RAG context retrieval, model-fallback for chat, scheduled daily insights/weekly summaries, and automated health report generation with cloud storage.\n\n* **Tools/API**\n  * New agent tools for semantic search, report generation/listing/reading, saving insights, and data-lake listing; `DATA_LAKE.put()` and `embedAndStore()` happen before the `agent_reports` insert. If that last write fails, the report is stored/searchable but `listReports()` can never discover it. Add compensating cleanup or write a D1 record first and finalize it after the external writes succeed.

## Code Review

This pull request introduces a comprehensive RAG-based health data agent system. Key additions include automated daily and weekly insight generation via cron triggers, a knowledge base powered by Cloudflare Vectorize for semantic search, and a reporting system that stores detailed Markdown analysis in R2. ## Pull request overview

This PR introduces a RAG-capable “data-agent” architecture on Cloudflare by routing Workers AI calls through an AI Gateway, adding Vectorize-backed semantic search over stored insights, persisting generated reports to R2, expanding D1 schema for agent data, and adding automated cron-driven insight generation plus a Vitest test suite.

**Changes:**
- Provision AI Gateway via Pulumi and route Workers AI through it (logging/cache/rate limiting).
- Add Vectorize + D1 knowledge base + new agent tools (semantic search, report generation/reading, insight saving, R2 listing).
- Add R2-backed report storage + cron-triggered daily/weekly generation, with Vitest tests for new modules.

1	import { AIChatAgent } from "@cloudflare/ai-chat";
2	import { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText } from "ai";
3	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
4	import { retrieveRelevantContext } from "./knowledge";
5	import { CHAT_MODELS } from "./models";
6	import { createTools, SYSTEM_PROMPT } from "./tools";
7	import type { Env } from "./types";
8	
9	export class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {
10	  async onChatMessage(
11	    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[0],
12	    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[1]
13	  ) {
14	    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });
15	
16	    // RAG: retrieve relevant context for the latest user message
17	    let ragContext = "";
18	    const lastUserMsg = this.messages.filter((m) => m.role === "user").at(-1);
19	    if (lastUserMsg?.parts) {
20	      const queryText = lastUserMsg.parts
21	        .filter((p): p is { type: "text"; text: string } => p.type === "text")
22	        .map((p) => p.text)
23	        .join(" ");
24	
25	      if (queryText) {
26	        const context = await retrieveRelevantContext(this.env, queryText, 3);
27	        if (context) {
28	          ragContext = `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${context}`;
29	        }
30	      }
31	    }
32	
33	    const streamOptions = {
34	      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT + ragContext,
35	      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
36	      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,
37	      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
38	      tools: createTools(this.env),
39	    };
40	
41	    // Model fallback: try each model in order
42	    let lastError: unknown;
43	    for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {
44	      try {
45	        const result = streamText({
46	          ...streamOptions,
47	          model: workersai(modelId),
48	        });
49	        return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
50	      } catch (error) {
51	        console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);
52	        lastError = error;
53	      }
54	    }
55	
56	    throw lastError ?? new Error("All chat models failed");
57	  }
58	}
59	
```

1	import { EMBEDDING_MODEL } from "./models";
2	
3	export type SearchResult = {
4	  id: string;
5	  content: string;
6	  contentType: string;
7	  score: number;
8	  metadata: Record<string, string> | null;
9	};
10	
11	export async function embedText(ai: Ai, text: string): Promise<number[]> {
12	  const result = (await ai.run(EMBEDDING_MODEL, { text: [text] })) as { data: number[][] };
13	  return result.data[0];
14	}
15	
16	export async function embedAndStore(
17	  ai: Ai,
18	  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,
19	  db: D1Database,
20	  content: string,
21	  contentType: string,
22	  metadata?: Record<string, string>
23	): Promise<string> {
24	  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
25	  const embedding = await embedText(ai, content);
26	
27	  await vectorize.upsert([
28	    {
29	      id,
30	      values: embedding,
31	      metadata: { content_type: contentType, ...metadata },
32	    },
33	  ]);
34	
35	  await db
36	    .prepare(
37	      "INSERT INTO agent_knowledge_base (id, content, content_type, metadata_json, vector_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
38	    )
39	    .bind(id, content, contentType, metadata ? JSON.stringify(metadata) : null, id)
40	    .run();
41	
42	  return id;
43	}
44	
45	export async function semanticSearch(
46	  ai: Ai,
47	  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,
48	  db: D1Database,
49	  query: string,
50	  topK = 5
51	): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
52	  const queryEmbedding = await embedText(ai, query);
53	  const matches = await vectorize.query(queryEmbedding, {
54	    topK,
55	    returnMetadata: "all",
56	  });
57	
58	  if (!matches.matches.length) {
59	    return [];
60	  }
61	
62	  const ids = matches.matches.map((m) => m.id);
63	  const placeholders = ids.map(() => "?").join(", ");
64	  const rows = await db
65	    .prepare(
66	      `SELECT id, content, content_type, metadata_json FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`
67	    )
68	    .bind(...ids)
69	    .all<{ id: string; content: string; content_type: string; metadata_json: string | null }>();
70	
71	  const contentMap = new Map(rows.results.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
72	
73	  return matches.matches
74	    .filter((m) => m.score > 0.6)
75	    .map((m) => {
76	      const row = contentMap.get(m.id);
77	      return {
78	        id: m.id,
79	        content: row?.content ?? "",
80	        contentType: row?.content_type ?? "",
81	        score: m.score,
82	        metadata: row?.metadata_json ? JSON.parse(row.metadata_json) : null,
83	      };
84	    });
85	}
86

1	import { embedAndStore, semanticSearch } from "./embeddings";
2	import { generateTextWithFallback } from "./models";
3	import type { Env } from "./types";
4	
5	const INSIGHT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Given the following health metrics, generate a concise insight in Japanese (2-3 sentences). Focus on trends, anomalies, or noteworthy patterns.`;
6	
7	export async function generateDailyInsight(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {
8	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
9	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories
10	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
11	     WHERE day = date('now', '-1 day')
12	     LIMIT 1`
13	  ).all<{
14	    day: string;
15	    sleep_score: number | null;
16	    activity_score: number | null;
17	    readiness_score: number | null;
18	    steps: number | null;
19	    total_calories: number | null;
20	  }>();
21	
22	  if (!results.length) return null;
23	
24	  const row = results[0];
25	  const { text: insight } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
26	    system: INSIGHT_PROMPT,
27	    prompt: `日付: ${row.day}\n睡眠スコア: ${row.sleep_score}\n活動スコア: ${row.activity_score}\nコンディション: ${row.readiness_score}\n歩数: ${row.steps}\n消費カロリー: ${row.total_calories}`,
28	  });
29	
30	  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, insight, "insight", {
31	    day: row.day,
32	    source: "daily_auto",
33	  });
34	
35	  return insight;
36	}
37	
38	export async function generateWeeklySummary(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {
39	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
40	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories
41	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
42	     WHERE day >= date('now', '-7 days')
43	     ORDER BY day`
44	  ).all<{
45	    day: string;
46	    sleep_score: number | null;
47	    activity_score: number | null;
48	    readiness_score: number | null;
49	    steps: number | null;
50	    total_calories: number | null;
51	  }>();
52	
53	  if (!results.length) return null;
54	
55	  const { text: summary } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
56	    system: `You are a health data analyst. Given a week of health metrics, write a comprehensive weekly summary in Japanese (4-6 sentences). Include averages, best/worst days, and trends.`,
57	    prompt: `週間データ:\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,
58	  });
59	
60	  const now = new Date();
61	  const weekStart = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString().split("T")[0];
62	  const weekEnd = new Date(now.getTime() - 86400000).toISOString().split("T")[0];
63	
64	  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, "weekly_summary", {
65	    period_start: weekStart,
66	    period_end: weekEnd,
67	    source: "weekly_auto",
68	  });
69	
70	  return summary;
71	}
72	
73	export async function retrieveRelevantContext(
74	  env: Env,
75	  query: string,
76	  topK = 3
77	): Promise<string | null> {
78	  const results = await semanticSearch(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, query, topK);
79	
80	  if (!results.length) return null;
81	
82	  return results.map((r) => `[${r.contentType}] ${r.content}`).join("\n\n");
83	}
84

1	import { tool } from "ai";
2	import { z } from "zod";
3	import { embedAndStore, semanticSearch } from "./embeddings";
4	import { generateHealthReport, listReports as queryReports } from "./reports";
5	import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
6	import type { Env } from "./types";
7	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
8	
9	export const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant with access to a knowledge base and analytics tools. You MUST use tools to answer every user question. Never refuse. Never say you cannot help.
10	
11	When the user asks about sleep, activity, readiness, or heart rate, generate a SQL SELECT query and call queryD1.
12	When the user asks about trends, patterns, or past insights, use semanticSearch first.
13	When the user asks for a report, use generateReport.
14	
15	Database schema (SQLite):
16	${D1_SCHEMA}
17	
18	SQL hints:
19	- "yesterday" = date('now', '-1 day')
20	- "this week" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')
21	- "this month" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')
22	- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view (has sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories)
23	- Always add LIMIT 30 for large queries
24	
25	Rules:
26	- ALWAYS call a tool. Do not respond without using a tool first.
27	- Only SELECT/WITH queries allowed for queryD1.
28	- Respond in Japanese.
29	- Summarize results concisely.
30	`;
31	
32	export function createTools(env: Env) {
33	  const { DB: db, AI: ai, VECTORIZE: vectorize, DATA_LAKE: dataLake } = env;
34	
35	  return {
36	    queryD1: tool({
37	      description:
38	        "SQLite (D1) データベースに SELECT クエリを実行してデータを取得する。ユーザーの質問に回答するために必ずこのツールを使うこと。",
39	      inputSchema: z.object({
40	        sql: z.string().describe("実行するSELECTクエリ"),
41	        params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("バインドパラメータ"),
42	      }),
43	      execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {
44	        if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
45	          return { success: false, error: "SELECT/WITH クエリのみ実行可能です。" };
46	        }
47	        try {
48	          const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
49	          const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;
50	          const queryResult = await bound.all();
51	
52	          return {
53	            success: true,
54	            rowCount: queryResult.results.length,
55	            results: queryResult.results.slice(0, 50),
56	          };
57	        } catch (error) {
58	          return {
59	            success: false,
60	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
61	          };
62	        }
63	      },
64	    }),
65	
66	    listTables: tool({
67	      description: "D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する",
68	      inputSchema: z.object({}),
69	      execute: async () => {
70	        const queryResult = await db
71	          .prepare(
72	            "SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name"
73	          )
74	          .all();
75	        return queryResult.results;
76	      },
77	    }),
78	
79	    semanticSearch: tool({
80	      description:
81	        "自然言語クエリで知識ベースを意味検索する。過去のインサイト、週次サマリー、パターンを検索できる。",
82	      inputSchema: z.object({
83	        query: z.string().describe("検索クエリ（自然言語）"),
84	        topK: z.number().optional().describe("返す結果数 (デフォルト: 5)"),
85	      }),
86	      execute: async ({ query, topK }) => {
87	        const results = await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, query, topK ?? 5);
88	        if (!results.length) {
89	          return {
90	            success: true,
91	            message: "関連するインサイトが見つかりませんでした。",
92	            results: [],
93	          };
94	        }
95	        return {
96	          success: true,
97	          resultCount: results.length,
98	          results: results.map((r) => ({
99	            contentType: r.contentType,
100	            content: r.content,
101	            score: Math.round(r.score * 100) / 100,
102	          })),
103	        };
104	      },
105	    }),
106	
107	    generateReport: tool({
108	      description: "指定期間の健康データレポートを生成してR2に保存する。",
109	      inputSchema: z.object({
110	        type: z.enum(["weekly_health", "monthly_health", "custom"]).describe("レポートタイプ"),
111	        periodStart: z.string().describe("開始日 (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
112	        periodEnd: z.string().describe("終了日 (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
113	      }),
114	      execute: async ({ type, periodStart, periodEnd }) => {
115	        try {
116	          const report = await generateHealthReport(env, type, periodStart, periodEnd);
117	          return {
118	            success: true,
119	            reportId: report.id,
120	            title: report.title,
121	            r2Key: report.r2Key,
122	            summary: report.summary,
123	          };
124	        } catch (error) {
125	          return {
126	            success: false,
127	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
128	          };
129	        }
130	      },
131	    }),
132	
133	    listReports: tool({
134	      description: "生成済みレポートの一覧を取得する。",
135	      inputSchema: z.object({
136	        type: z
137	          .enum(["weekly_health", "monthly_health", "custom"])
138	          .optional()
139	          .describe("レポートタイプ"),
140	        limit: z.number().optional().describe("取得件数 (デフォルト: 10)"),
141	      }),
142	      execute: async ({ type, limit }) => {
143	        return await queryReports(db, type, limit ?? 10);
144	      },
145	    }),
146	
147	    readReport: tool({
148	      description: "R2に保存されたレポートの全文を取得する。",
149	      inputSchema: z.object({
150	        reportId: z.string().describe("レポートID"),
151	      }),
152	      execute: async ({ reportId }) => {
153	        const row = await db
154	          .prepare("SELECT r2_key, title FROM agent_reports WHERE id = ?")
155	          .bind(reportId)
156	          .first<{ r2_key: string; title: string }>();
157	
158	        if (!row) {
159	          return { success: false, error: "レポートが見つかりません。" };
160	        }
161	
162	        const obj = await dataLake.get(row.r2_key);
163	        if (!obj) {
164	          return { success: false, error: "R2からレポートを取得できませんでした。" };
165	        }
166	
167	        const content = await obj.text();
168	        return { success: true, title: row.title, content };
169	      },
170	    }),
171	
172	    saveInsight: tool({

177	        metadata: z.record(z.string(), z.string()).optional().describe("メタデータ (任意)"),
178	      }),
179	      execute: async ({ content, contentType, metadata }) => {
180	        try {

182	          return { success: true, id, message: "インサイトを保存しました。" };
183	        } catch (error) {
184	          return {
185	            success: false,
186	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
187	          };
188	        }
189	      },
190	    }),
191	
192	    listDataLakeFiles: tool({
193	      description:
194	        "R2データレイクのファイル一覧を取得する。Parquetファイルやレポートの確認に使う。",

197	        limit: z.number().optional().describe("取得件数 (デフォルト: 20)"),
198	      }),

201	        return {

207	            uploaded: o.uploaded.toISOString(),
208	          })),
209	        };

1	import { routeAgentRequest } from "agents";
2	import { generateDailyInsight, generateWeeklySummary } from "./knowledge";
3	import { handleSlackEvent } from "./slack";
4	import type { Env } from "./types";
5	
6	export { DataAgent } from "./agent";
7	
8	export default {
9	  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
10	    const url = new URL(request.url);
11	
12	    if (url.pathname === "/health") {
13	      return new Response("OK");
14	    }
15	
16	    // Slack Events API
17	    if (url.pathname === "/slack/events" && request.method === "POST") {
18	      // Pass waitUntil so Slack handler can process AI response in background
19	      globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL = (p: Promise<unknown>) => ctx.waitUntil(p);
20	      return handleSlackEvent(request, env);
21	    }
22	
23	    // Agent WebSocket routing
24	    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);
25	    if (agentResponse) {
26	      return agentResponse;
27	    }
28	
29	    return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
30	  },
31	
32	  async scheduled(_event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
33	    // Daily: generate insight from yesterday's health data
34	    ctx.waitUntil(
35	      generateDailyInsight(env).then((insight) => {
36	        if (insight) {
37	          console.log("Daily insight generated:", insight.substring(0, 100));
38	        }
39	      })
40	    );
41	
42	    // Weekly (Monday): generate weekly summary
43	    const today = new Date();
44	    if (today.getUTCDay() === 1) {
45	      ctx.waitUntil(
46	        generateWeeklySummary(env).then((summary) => {
47	          if (summary) {
48	            console.log("Weekly summary generated:", summary.substring(0, 100));
49	          }
50	        })
51	      );
52	    }
53	  },
54	};
55

1	import { embedAndStore } from "./embeddings";
2	import { generateTextWithFallback } from "./models";
3	import type { Env } from "./types";
4	
5	const REPORT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Generate a detailed health report in Japanese Markdown format.
6	Include:
7	- 概要 (1-2 sentences summary)
8	- 睡眠分析 (scores, trends, best/worst days)
9	- 活動分析 (steps, calories, activity scores)
10	- コンディション分析 (readiness scores, temperature)
11	- まとめと改善提案
12	
13	Use headers (##), bullet points, and bold for emphasis.`;
14	
15	export type ReportResult = {
16	  id: string;
17	  title: string;
18	  r2Key: string;
19	  summary: string;
20	};
21	
22	export async function generateHealthReport(
23	  env: Env,
24	  type: string,
25	  periodStart: string,
26	  periodEnd: string
27	): Promise<ReportResult> {
28	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
29	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score,
30	            steps, total_calories, active_calories, temperature_deviation,
31	            sleep_deep_sleep, sleep_efficiency, sleep_total_sleep
32	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
33	     WHERE day >= ? AND day <= ?
34	     ORDER BY day`
35	  )
36	    .bind(periodStart, periodEnd)
37	    .all();
38	
39	  if (!results.length) {
40	    throw new Error(`期間 ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd} のデータが見つかりません。`);
41	  }
42	
43	  // Generate full report (with model fallback)
44	  const { text: reportContent } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
45	    system: REPORT_PROMPT,
46	    prompt: `期間: ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd}\nデータ件数: ${results.length}日分\n\nデータ:\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,
47	  });
48	
49	  // Generate concise summary for embedding (with model fallback)
50	  const { text: summary } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
51	    system: "Summarize the following health report in 2-3 sentences in Japanese.",
52	    prompt: reportContent,
53	  });
54	
55	  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
56	  const r2Key = `reports/${type}/${periodStart}_${periodEnd}.md`;
57	  const title = `${type === "weekly_health" ? "週次" : type === "monthly_health" ? "月次" : "カスタム"}ヘルスレポート (${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd})`;
58	
59	  // Store full report in R2
60	  await env.DATA_LAKE.put(r2Key, reportContent, {
61	    customMetadata: { report_id: id, type, period_start: periodStart, period_end: periodEnd },
62	  });
63	
64	  // Embed summary in Vectorize
65	  const vectorId = await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, "report", {
66	    report_id: id,
67	    period_start: periodStart,
68	    period_end: periodEnd,
69	  });
70	
71	  // Store metadata in D1
72	  await env.DB.prepare(
73	    `INSERT INTO agent_reports (id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, r2_key, summary, vector_id)
74	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
75	  )
76	    .bind(id, type, title, periodStart, periodEnd, r2Key, summary, vectorId)
77	    .run();
78	
79	  return { id, title, r2Key, summary };
80	}
81	
82	export async function listReports(
83	  db: D1Database,
84	  type?: string,
85	  limit = 10
86	): Promise<{ results: unknown[] }> {
87	  const query = type
88	    ? "SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports WHERE report_type = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?"
89	    : "SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?";
90	
91	  const stmt = type ? db.prepare(query).bind(type, limit) : db.prepare(query).bind(limit);
92	  const result = await stmt.all();
93	  return { results: result.results };
94	}
95

1	import { retrieveRelevantContext } from "./knowledge";
2	import { generateTextWithFallback } from "./models";
3	import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
4	import type { Env } from "./types";
5	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
6	
7	const SQL_GEN_PROMPT = `You are a SQL generator for a SQLite (D1) database containing Oura Ring health data.
8	Given a user question, output ONLY a valid SELECT SQL query. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks. Just the raw SQL.
9	
10	Database schema:
11	${D1_SCHEMA}
12	
13	SQL hints:
14	- "yesterday" = date('now', '-1 day')
15	- "this week" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')
16	- "this month" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')
17	- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view
18	- Always add LIMIT 30
19	- Date columns are TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD format)
20	
21	Output ONLY the SQL query. Nothing else.`;
22	
23	const SUMMARIZE_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant. Given the user's question and query results from an Oura Ring database, provide a concise answer in Japanese.
24	If no data is returned, say "データが見つかりませんでした。"
25	Keep the answer short and helpful.`;
26	
27	type SlackEvent =
28	  | { type: "url_verification"; challenge: string }
29	  | {
30	      type: "event_callback";
31	      event: {
32	        type: string;
33	        text?: string;
34	        user?: string;
35	        channel?: string;
36	        bot_id?: string;
37	        ts?: string;
38	        thread_ts?: string;
39	      };
40	    };
41	
42	async function verifySlackSignature(
43	  request: Request,
44	  body: string,
45	  signingSecret: string
46	): Promise<boolean> {
47	  const timestamp = request.headers.get("x-slack-request-timestamp");
48	  const signature = request.headers.get("x-slack-signature");
49	  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;
50	
51	  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes
52	  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;
53	
54	  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;
55	  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
56	    "raw",
57	    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),
58	    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
59	    false,
60	    ["sign"]
61	  );
62	  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));
63	  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
64	  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;
65	}
66	
67	async function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {
68	  const response = await fetch("https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage", {
69	    method: "POST",
70	    headers: {
71	      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
72	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
73	    },
74	    body: JSON.stringify({
75	      channel,
76	      text,
77	      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),
78	    }),
79	  });
80	
81	  if (!response.ok) {
82	    console.error("Slack API HTTP error:", response.status, response.statusText);
83	  } else {
84	    const result = (await response.json()) as { ok: boolean; error?: string };
85	    if (!result.ok) {
86	      console.error("Slack API error:", result.error);
87	    }
88	  }
89	}
90	
91	function extractSql(raw: string): string {
92	  // Strip markdown code blocks if present
93	  let sql = raw
94	    .replace(/```sql\n?/gi, "")
95	    .replace(/```\n?/g, "")
96	    .trim();
97	  // Take only first statement
98	  const semicolonIdx = sql.indexOf(";");
99	  if (semicolonIdx > 0) {
100	    sql = sql.substring(0, semicolonIdx + 1);
101	  }
102	  return sql;
103	}
104	
105	export async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
106	  const body = await request.text();
107	
108	  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {
109	    return new Response("Invalid signature", { status: 401 });
110	  }
111	
112	  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);
113	
114	  // Slack URL verification challenge
115	  if (event.type === "url_verification") {
116	    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" } });
117	  }
118	
119	  if (event.type !== "event_callback") {
120	    return new Response("OK");
121	  }
122	
123	  const { event: slackEvent } = event;
124	
125	  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops
126	  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== "app_mention") {
127	    return new Response("OK");
128	  }
129	
130	  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, "").trim();
131	  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {
132	    return new Response("OK");
133	  }
134	
135	  const channel = slackEvent.channel;
136	  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;
137	
138	  const promise = (async () => {
139	    try {
140	      // Step 1: Generate SQL from user question (with model fallback)
141	      const { text: rawSql } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
142	        system: SQL_GEN_PROMPT,
143	        prompt: userText,
144	      });
145	
146	      const sql = extractSql(rawSql);
147	
148	      if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
149	        await postSlackMessage(
150	          env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
151	          channel,
152	          "SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。",
153	          threadTs
154	        );
155	        return;
156	      }
157	
158	      // Step 2: Execute SQL
159	      const queryResult = await env.DB.prepare(sql).all();
160	      const rows = queryResult.results.slice(0, 50);
161	
162	      // Step 2.5: Retrieve relevant context from knowledge base for RAG
163	      const ragResults = await retrieveRelevantContext(env, userText, 2);
164	      const contextBlock = ragResults ? `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${ragResults}` : "";
165	
166	      // Step 3: Summarize results with RAG context (truncate to avoid exceeding model context limits)
167	      const rowsPreview =
168	        rows.length > 10 ? [...rows.slice(0, 10), `... and ${rows.length - 10} more rows`] : rows;
169	      const { text: answer } = await generateTextWithFallback(env.AI, {
170	        system: SUMMARIZE_PROMPT + contextBlock,
171	        prompt: `ユーザーの質問: ${userText}\n\n実行したSQL: ${sql}\n\nクエリ結果 (${queryResult.results.length}件):\n${JSON.stringify(rowsPreview, null, 2)}`,
172	      });
173	
174	      const message = `${answer}\n\n\`\`\`${sql}\`\`\``;
175	      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, channel, message, threadTs);
176	    } catch (error) {
177	      console.error("Slack handler error:", error);
178	      await postSlackMessage(
179	        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
180	        channel,
181	        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
182	        threadTs
183	      );
184	    }
185	  })();
186	
187	  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);
188	
189	  return new Response("OK");
190	}
191

1	export const D1_SCHEMA = `
2	-- ============ Oura Ring データ ============
3	
4	-- oura_daily_sleep: 日別睡眠データ
5	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD), score INTEGER (睡眠スコア 0-100)
6	--   timestamp TEXT, deep_sleep INTEGER, efficiency INTEGER, latency INTEGER
7	--   rem_sleep INTEGER, restfulness INTEGER, timing INTEGER, total_sleep INTEGER
8	--   synced_at TEXT
9	
10	-- oura_daily_activity: 日別活動データ
11	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT, score INTEGER (活動スコア 0-100)
12	--   active_calories INTEGER, total_calories INTEGER, steps INTEGER
13	--   equivalent_walking_distance REAL
14	--   high_activity_time INTEGER, medium_activity_time INTEGER
15	--   low_activity_time INTEGER, sedentary_time INTEGER, resting_time INTEGER
16	--   met_average REAL
17	--   meet_daily_targets INTEGER, move_every_hour INTEGER, recovery_time INTEGER
18	--   stay_active INTEGER, training_frequency INTEGER, training_volume INTEGER
19	--   timestamp TEXT, synced_at TEXT
20	
21	-- oura_daily_readiness: 日別コンディション
22	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT, score INTEGER (コンディションスコア 0-100)
23	--   temperature_deviation REAL, temperature_trend_deviation REAL
24	--   timestamp TEXT
25	--   activity_balance INTEGER, body_temperature INTEGER
26	--   hrv_balance INTEGER, previous_day_activity INTEGER
27	--   previous_night INTEGER, recovery_index INTEGER
28	--   resting_heart_rate INTEGER, sleep_balance INTEGER
29	--   synced_at TEXT
30	
31	-- oura_heart_rate: 心拍数 (5分間隔、1日約288レコード)
32	--   id INTEGER PK AUTOINCREMENT, bpm INTEGER, source TEXT, timestamp TEXT, day TEXT
33	--   synced_at TEXT
34	
35	-- v_oura_daily_summary: Oura日次サマリービュー (集計済み)
36	--   day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score
37	--   steps, total_calories, active_calories
38	--   temperature_deviation
39	--   sleep_deep_sleep, sleep_efficiency, sleep_total_sleep
40	
41	-- 注意: 日付は TEXT 型 (ISO 8601)。SQLite の date() / strftime() を使うこと。
42	
43	-- ============ Agent Tables ============
44	
45	-- agent_knowledge_base: 知識ベース (埋め込み済みインサイト)
46	--   id TEXT PK, content TEXT, content_type TEXT ('weekly_summary'|'insight'|'pattern')
47	--   metadata_json TEXT (JSON), vector_id TEXT, created_at TEXT, updated_at TEXT
48	
49	-- agent_reports: 生成レポートメタデータ
50	--   id TEXT PK, report_type TEXT ('weekly_health'|'monthly_health'|'custom')
51	--   title TEXT, period_start TEXT, period_end TEXT
52	--   r2_key TEXT (R2オブジェクトキー), summary TEXT, vector_id TEXT, created_at TEXT
53	
54	-- agent_conversation_logs: 会話履歴
55	--   id TEXT PK, session_id TEXT, source TEXT ('chat'|'slack')
56	--   user_message TEXT, assistant_response TEXT, tools_used TEXT (JSON)
57	--   model_id TEXT, created_at TEXT
58	`;
59

1	import { generateText } from "ai";
2	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
3	
4	/**
5	 * チャットモデル: 高性能→コスト効率→軽量の順にフォールバック
6	 * - gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:  $0.100/M input, $0.300/M output (256K ctx, Reasoning, Vision, Function calling)
7	 * - qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8:   $0.051/M input, $0.335/M output (最安 30B クラス)
8	 * - llama-3-8b-instruct-awq: $0.123/M input, $0.266/M output (8B 最軽量フォールバック)
9	 */
10	export const CHAT_MODELS = [
11	  "@cf/google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it",
12	  "@cf/qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8",
13	  "@cf/meta/llama-3-8b-instruct-awq",
14	] as const;
15	
16	/**
17	 * 埋め込みモデル: 384次元、$0.020/M input (最安クラス)
18	 */
19	export const EMBEDDING_MODEL = "@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5" as const;
20	export const EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS = 384;
21	
22	/**
23	 * generateText をモデルフォールバック付きで実行する。
24	 * CHAT_MODELS を順に試し、最初に成功したものを返す。
25	 */
26	export async function generateTextWithFallback(
27	  ai: Ai,
28	  options: { system?: string; prompt: string }
29	): Promise<{ text: string }> {
30	  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: ai });
31	  let lastError: unknown;
32	
33	  for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {
34	    try {
35	      const result = await generateText({
36	        model: workersai(modelId),
37	        system: options.system,
38	        prompt: options.prompt,
39	      });
40	      return { text: result.text };
41	    } catch (error) {
42	      console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);
43	      lastError = error;
44	    }
45	  }
46	
47	  throw lastError ?? new Error("All chat models failed");
48	}
49	
```

1	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
2	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
3	
4	describe("isSafeQuery", () => {
5	  describe("許可されるクエリ", () => {
6	    it("SELECTクエリを許可する", () => {
7	      expect(isSafeQuery("SELECT * FROM users")).toBe(true);
8	    });
9	
10	    it("WITHクエリ (CTE) を許可する", () => {
11	      expect(isSafeQuery("WITH cte AS (SELECT 1) SELECT * FROM cte")).toBe(true);
12	    });
13	
14	    it("末尾にセミコロンがあるSELECTクエリを許可する", () => {
15	      expect(isSafeQuery("SELECT 1;")).toBe(true);
16	    });
17	
18	    it("小文字のselectを許可する", () => {
19	      expect(isSafeQuery("select day, score from oura_daily_sleep")).toBe(true);
20	    });
21	
22	    it("前後に空白があるクエリを許可する", () => {
23	      expect(isSafeQuery("  SELECT 1  ")).toBe(true);
24	    });
25	  });
26	
27	  describe("拒否されるクエリ", () => {
28	    it("INSERTクエリを拒否する", () => {
29	      expect(isSafeQuery("INSERT INTO users VALUES (1)")).toBe(false);
30	    });
31	
32	    it("UPDATEクエリを拒否する", () => {
33	      expect(isSafeQuery("UPDATE users SET name = 'x'")).toBe(false);
34	    });
35	
36	    it("DELETEクエリを拒否する", () => {
37	      expect(isSafeQuery("DELETE FROM users")).toBe(false);
38	    });
39	
40	    it("DROP TABLEを拒否する", () => {
41	      expect(isSafeQuery("DROP TABLE users")).toBe(false);
42	    });
43	
44	    it("複数ステートメント (SQLインジェクション) を拒否する", () => {
45	      expect(isSafeQuery("SELECT 1; DROP TABLE users")).toBe(false);
46	    });
47	
48	    it("空文字列を拒否する", () => {
49	      expect(isSafeQuery("")).toBe(false);
50	    });
51	  });
52	});
53

71	
72	    expect(result).toBeNull();

74	    expect(embedAndStore).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
75	  });
76	});

1	/**
2	 * Validates that a SQL query is safe to execute (SELECT/WITH only, single statement).
3	 */
4	export function isSafeQuery(sql: string): boolean {
5	  const trimmed = sql.trim();
6	  const normalized = trimmed.toUpperCase();
7	
8	  // Only allow SELECT or WITH queries
9	  if (!normalized.startsWith("SELECT") && !normalized.startsWith("WITH")) {
10	    return false;
11	  }
12	
13	  // Reject multi-statement queries (semicolon not at the end)
14	  const semicolonIdx = trimmed.indexOf(";");
15	  if (semicolonIdx !== -1 && semicolonIdx !== trimmed.length - 1) {
16	    return false;
17	  }
18	
19	  return true;
20	}
21

1	-- ============================================
2	-- Agent: Knowledge Base (embedded insights for RAG)
3	-- ============================================
4	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_knowledge_base (
5	    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
6	    content TEXT NOT NULL,
7	    content_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_summary', 'insight', 'pattern'
8	    metadata_json TEXT,
9	    vector_id TEXT,
10	    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
11	    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
12	);
13	
14	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_type
15	  ON agent_knowledge_base(content_type);
16	
17	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_vector
18	  ON agent_knowledge_base(vector_id);
19	
20	-- ============================================
21	-- Agent: Generated Reports
22	-- ============================================
23	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_reports (
24	    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
25	    report_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_health', 'monthly_health', 'custom'
26	    title TEXT NOT NULL,
27	    period_start TEXT,
28	    period_end TEXT,
29	    r2_key TEXT,
30	    summary TEXT,
31	    vector_id TEXT,
32	    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
33	);
34	
35	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_reports_type
36	  ON agent_reports(report_type, period_start DESC);
37	
38	-- ============================================
39	-- Agent: Conversation Logs
40	-- ============================================
41	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_conversation_logs (
42	    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
43	    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
44	    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',  -- 'chat', 'slack'
45	    user_message TEXT NOT NULL,
46	    assistant_response TEXT,
47	    tools_used TEXT,
48	    model_id TEXT,
49	    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
50	);
51	
52	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_session
53	  ON agent_conversation_logs(session_id, created_at DESC);
54	
55	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_created
56	  ON agent_conversation_logs(created_at DESC);

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"github.com/pulumi/pulumi-cloudflare/sdk/v5/go/cloudflare"
5		"github.com/pulumi/pulumi/sdk/v3/go/pulumi"
6	)
7	
8	type CloudflareOutputs struct {
9		D1DatabaseId   pulumi.IDOutput
10		D1DatabaseName pulumi.StringOutput
11		R2BucketName   pulumi.StringOutput
12		KvNamespaceId  pulumi.IDOutput
13		AiGatewayId    pulumi.StringOutput
14	}
15	
16	func createCloudflareResources(ctx *pulumi.Context, accountId string) (*CloudflareOutputs, error) {
17		// ===========================================
18		// D1 Database
19		// ===========================================
20		// 既存のD1データベースをインポートして管理
21		// pulumi import cloudflare:index/d1Database:D1Database raw <account_id>/<database_id>
22		rawDb, err := cloudflare.NewD1Database(ctx, "raw", &cloudflare.D1DatabaseArgs{
23			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
24			Name:      pulumi.String("raw"),
25		}, pulumi.Protect(true))
26		if err != nil {
27			return nil, err
28		}
29	
30		// ===========================================
31		// R2 Bucket (for data lake)
32		// ===========================================
33		dataLake, err := cloudflare.NewR2Bucket(ctx, "data-lake", &cloudflare.R2BucketArgs{
34			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
35			Name:      pulumi.String("data-lake"),
36			Location:  pulumi.String("APAC"),
37		})
38		if err != nil {
39			return nil, err
40		}
41	
42		// ===========================================
43		// Workers KV Namespace (for caching)
44		// ===========================================
45		cacheKv, err := cloudflare.NewWorkersKvNamespace(ctx, "data-cache", &cloudflare.WorkersKvNamespaceArgs{
46			AccountId: pulumi.String(accountId),
47			Title:     pulumi.String("data-cache"),
48		})
49		if err != nil {
50			return nil, err
51		}
52	
53		// ===========================================
54		// AI Gateway (for data-agent)
55		// ===========================================
56		aiGateway, err := cloudflare.NewAiGateway(ctx, "data-agent-gateway", &cloudflare.AiGatewayArgs{
57			AccountId:              pulumi.String(accountId),
58			Id:                     pulumi.String("data-agent-gateway"),
59			CacheInvalidateOnUpdate: pulumi.Bool(true),
60			CacheTtl:               pulumi.Int(3600),
61			CollectLogs:            pulumi.Bool(true),
62			RateLimitingLimit:      pulumi.Int(100),
63			RateLimitingInterval:   pulumi.Int(60),
64			RateLimitingTechnique:  pulumi.String("fixed"),
65		})
66		if err != nil {
67			return nil, err
68		}
69	
70		return &CloudflareOutputs{
71			D1DatabaseId:   rawDb.ID(),
72			D1DatabaseName: rawDb.Name,
73			R2BucketName:   dataLake.Name,
74			KvNamespaceId:  cacheKv.ID(),
75			AiGatewayId:    aiGateway.ID().ToStringOutput(),
76		}, nil
77	}
78

  "old_string": "  const ids = matches.matches.map((m) => m.id);\n  const placeholders = ids.map(() => \"?\").join(\", \");\n  const rows = await db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, content, content_type, metadata_json FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`\n    )\n    .bind(...ids)\n    .all<{ id: string; content: string; content_type: string; metadata_json: string | null }>();\n\n  const contentMap = new Map(rows.results.map((r) => [r.id, r]));\n\n  return matches.matches\n    .filter((m) => m.score > 0.6)\n    .map((m) => {\n      const row = contentMap.get(m.id);\n      return {\n        id: m.id,\n        content: row?.content ?? \"\",\n        contentType: row?.content_type ?? \"\",\n        score: m.score,\n        metadata: row?.metadata_json ? JSON.parse(row.metadata_json) : null,\n      };\n    });",
  "new_string": "  // Filter by score before D1 query to avoid unnecessary reads\n  const relevantMatches = matches.matches.filter((m) => m.score > 0.6);\n  if (!relevantMatches.length) {\n    return [];\n  }\n\n  const ids = relevantMatches.map((m) => m.id);\n  const placeholders = ids.map(() => \"?\").join(\", \");\n  const rows = await db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, content, content_type, metadata_json FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`\n    )\n    .bind(...ids)\n    .all<{ id: string; content: string; content_type: string; metadata_json: string | null }>();\n\n  const contentMap = new Map(rows.results.map((r) => [r.id, r]));\n\n  return relevantMatches\n    .filter((m) => contentMap.has(m.id))\n    .map((m) => {\n      const row = contentMap.get(m.id)!;\n      let metadata: Record<string, string> | null = null;\n      if (row.metadata_json) {\n        try {\n          metadata = JSON.parse(row.metadata_json);\n        } catch {\n          console.error(`Failed to parse metadata_json for id=${m.id}`);\n        }\n      }\n      return {\n        id: m.id,\n        content: row.content,\n        contentType: row.content_type,\n        score: m.score,\n        metadata,\n      };\n    });"
}
